# Country profiles — Western Hemisphere

IMF World Economic Outlook, IMF Financial Soundness Indicators and credit-to-GDP gaps (BIS, or IMF Monetary and Financial Statistics for Kuwait, UAE, Qatar and Oman) for 40 economies in Western Hemisphere. Each country has its own section below. Built 2026-09-28 from github.com/Aknarik/country-data.

## Argentina — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

## Brazil — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Brazil: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▂▂▃▅▅█▅▄▃▅▄ (min -2.3, max 4.2).

## Colombia — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Colombia: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## Mexico — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Mexico: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▂▁▁▄▆█▇▆▆▆▅ (min -4.7, max -2.1).

## United States — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap
